def corr_weights(a, w):
    # w length 2n-1 (nonnegative weights sum 1 maybe)
    # returns grad length n = 2 * sum_k w_k a_{k-j} within range
    n=len(a); m=3*n-2; M=nextpow2(m); A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],M); W=np.fft.rfft(w,M); return 2*np.fft.irfft(A*W,M)[n-1:2*n-1]

Need compute score and gradient efficiently:
```
def fourier_obj(theta, n, M, beta):
   C = np.zeros(n//2+1, complex)
   C[1:M+1] = theta[0::2] + 1j*theta[1::2]
   phi = np.fft.irfft(C, n)
   # phi mean maybe 0; softmax
   phi_max = phi.max()
   exp_phi = np.exp(phi - phi_max)
   Z = exp_phi.sum()
   p = exp_phi / Z
   c = fast_conv(p)  # length 2n-1
   cmax = c.max()
   scaled = beta*(c-cmax)
   ez = np.exp(scaled)
   L = cmax + np.log(ez.sum())/beta
   w = ez/ez.sum()
   F = 2*n*L
   gP = convolve_weights(p,w)  # derivative of L w.r.t p (not F? F factor 2n constant)
   # project to tangent sum0: gP_mean = gP.dot(p)
   gPhi = p * (gP - np.dot(p,gP))
   gradC = np.fft.rfft(gPhi)
   grad_theta = np.empty(2*M)
   grad_theta[0::2] = gradC[1:M+1].real
   grad_theta[1::2] = gradC[1:M+1].imag
   return F, grad_theta
```

Need ensure convolve_weights p,w uses p and w. Let's implement top-level:
```
def cross_conv_weights(p, w):
   n = len(p)
   total = 3*n - 2
   M = 1 << (total -1).bit_length()
   Rp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M)
   Rw = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
   return 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(Rp * Rw, M)[n-1:2*n-1]
```

`fourier_obj(theta, n, M, beta)`:
- If M<=0: theta len0; C zeros; p uniform. But we need M>0.
- A=theta[0::2]; B=theta[1::2]. If len mismatch.
- C = np.zeros(n//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
- C[1:M+1] = 0.5*n*(A - 1j*B)
- phi = np.fft.irfft(C,n)
- p, exp? We need exp for later? gradient uses p.
- c=fast_conv(p)
- cmax=c.max(); scaled=beta*(c-cmax); ez=np.exp(scaled); z=ez.sum(); L=cmax + np.log(z)/beta; w=ez/z
- gP = cross_conv_weights(p,w)
- mean = float(gP @ p)
- gPhi = p*(gP - mean)
- G = np.fft.rfft(gPhi)
- grad = np.empty(2*M)
- grad[0::2] = G[1:M+1].real
- grad[1::2] = -G[1:M+1].imag
- score=2.0*n*L
- return score, grad

Need ensure G components scale? A^T g as sum; correct. To be safe, we can do numerical check mentally with single mode. Suppose p uniform, β high, gP maybe? The gradient sign. def mirror_descent(p, beta, eta, max_iters, deadline, score_lb=1.0):
    n=len(p); p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=p.sum()
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=exact_score_p(p,n)
    logp=np.log(p+1e-300)
    # maybe initialize logp stable
    base_eta=eta
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(p); cmax=c.max(); current=2n*cmax
       if current<best_score: best=current; best_p=p.copy()
       beta_cur = interpolate beta_start to beta_end over iters (or keep)
       scaled=beta_cur*(c-cmax); ez=exp; w=ez/sum
       g = cross_conv_weights(p,w)
       center = float(g @ p)
       g_c = g - center
       gscale = np.max(np.abs(g_c))
       if gscale <1e-30: break
       base = g_c / gscale
       # try line search with etas: [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1, eta*2]
       best_sc = current; best_p_trial=p
       for fac in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1,2,4?]:
           eta_cur=eta*fac
           logp_trial = logp - eta_cur*base
           logp_trial -= logp_trial.max()
           e=np.exp(logp_trial); s=e.sum(); if s<=0...
           p_trial=e/s
           sc=exact_score_p(p_trial,n)
           if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_p_trial=p_trial; best_eta=eta_cur
       if best_p_trial is not p:
           p=best_p_trial; logp=np.log(p+eps)-np.log(p).max? recompute stable
       # adaptive eta: if best fac was small, decrease; if large, increase
       eta = max(eta_min, min(eta_max, best_eta*1.1?))
```

Helper `optimize_full_log(p_init, beta_list, deadline, log_bound)` top-level returns best p, sc. It will run L-BFGS from p_init with each beta, updating p. It needs function `logp_objective(theta,beta)` top-level. It should keep best across betas.
```
def optimize_full_log(p_init, beta_list, deadline, log_bound=12.0, maxfun_high=8000):
    n=len(p_init); p=p_init.copy()
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_p(p,n)
    for beta in beta_list:
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       p_safe=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
       theta=np.clip(np.log(p_safe[1:]) - np.log(p_safe[0]), -log_bound, log_bound)
       bounds=[(-log_bound,log_bound)]*(n-1)
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       maxfun=int(min(20000, max(500, remaining/0.05))) # but estimate
       try: res=minimize(logp_objective, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, args=(beta,), options=...)
       except: pass
       p_try=np.exp? from res.x: phi[0]=0, phi[1:]=res.x; p_try=softmax(log_bound?)
       sc=exact_score_p(p_try,n); if sc<best_sc: best=p_try; best_sc=sc
       p=p_try.copy()
    return best,best_sc
```

Mirror descent details robust:
```
def mirror_descent(p_init, beta=None, eta_start=0.25, eta_end=0.03, max_iters=20000, deadline=None, verbose=False):
   n=len(p_init); p=normalize
   best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=exact_score
   logp=log(p+1e-300); logp -= max
   eta=eta_start
   last_best=best_sc
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if deadline and now>deadline: break
      c=fast_conv(p); cmax=max; sc=2n*cmax
      if sc<best_sc: best...
      # beta dynamic if None
      if beta is None: beta_cur = p_beta_auto(cmax)
      else: beta_cur=beta
      scaled=beta_cur*(c-cmax); ez=exp; w=ez/ez.sum()
      g = cross_conv_weights(p,w)
      center = float(g @ p)
      g_c = g - center
      scale = np.max(np.abs(g_c))
      if scale <1e-30: break
      base = g_c/scale
      # line search: try up to 3 etas
      candidates_eta = [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25]
      best_trial_sc=current; best_trial_p=None; chosen_eta=eta*0.25
      for f in [1.0,0.5,0.25,2.0]:
          et=eta*f
          if et<1e-4: continue
          lp=logp - et*base
          lp -= lp.max()
          e=np.exp(lp); s=e.sum()
          if s<=0: continue
          pt=e/s
          sc2=exact_score_p(pt,n)
          if sc2 < best_trial_sc - 1e-15:
             best_trial_sc=sc2; best_trial_p=pt; chosen_eta=et
      if best_trial_p is not None:
          p=best_trial_p
          # choose eta: adapt based on factor. If factor was 1 or 2, increase; if 0.25, decrease
          # We can record multiplier used: mults list. Simpler: if chosen_eta>eta*0.9: eta=min(eta*1.2, eta_max); else eta=max(eta*0.7, eta_min)
          if chosen_eta > eta:
              eta=min(eta*1.25+1e-3, eta_max)
          elif chosen_eta < 0.5*eta:
              eta=max(eta*0.75, eta_min)
          # anneal toward eta_end
          eta = max(eta_end, min(eta_start, eta*0.999?))
          logp=np.log(p+1e-300); logp -= logp.max()
      else:
          eta=max(eta*0.7, eta_min)
          if eta<eta_min: break? maybe still random perturbation.
   return best_p,best_sc
```
Line search exact_score uses fast_conv; we already computed c for current. Candidate evaluation cost. The `base` normalized by max, so eta dimensionless. If eta max 2, log ratio changes up to e^2=7.4, maybe too huge. Start 0.2. For polishing, small. Use eta_start=0.15, eta_end=0.01, eta_min=0.002, eta_max=1.5.

stagnant=0; if no best update >50: add small random smooth log perturbation; stagnant=0
```

`build_fourier_bounds(M, base)`:

Top-level imports:
```
import numpy as np

No import evaluate_sequence.

Helpers:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    if x <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(x)))
```
But `np.log2` overhead; use `(x-1).bit_length()`.
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_p`:
```
def normalize_p(arr):
   a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return None
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return None
   return a/s
```

`resample_p`:
```
def resample_p(p,n):
   if p is None or len(p)==0: return None
   if len(p)==n: p=out
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(p)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   vals=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
   vals=np.maximum(vals,0)
   s=vals.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
   return vals/s
```

`softmax_from_phi(phi)`:
```
def softmax_from_phi(phi):
   m=float(np.max(phi))
   if not np.isfinite(m): return np.ones(len(phi))/len(phi)
   e=np.exp(phi-m)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
   return e/s
```

`exact_score_p(p,n)` using fast_conv. If p sum not 1, still formula? We'll use normalized. But if p sum not 1 due resample error, use S. For p sum1, 2n max c. Use S to be safe:
```
def exact_score_p(a,n):
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-300: return inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    return 2*n*max(c)/S*S
```

For max, negative not matter. For weights, c maybe slight negative; okay. Could c=np.where(c>0,c,0) but if all negative? No. Use np.clip(c,0,None) for safety.
```
Clip to 0 might slightly bias but okay. For LSE, better not clip? Max nonnegative. Use `c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)`.

Now objectives:
`fourier_phi_from_theta(theta,n,M)`:
```
def theta_to_phi(theta,n,M):
   C=np.zeros(n//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
   m=min(M,n//2)
   if m>0:
      C[1:m+1] = 0.5*n*(theta[0:2*m:2] - 1j*theta[1:2*m:2])
   phi=np.fft.irfft(C,n)
   return phi
```

`theta_to_p` uses softmax.

`fourier_objective(theta, n, M, beta)`:
Need M may > n//2? Use effective M_eff = min(M,n//2). But theta length 2*M; if M_eff < M ignore extras. In bounds we ensure M<=n//2. We'll pass valid.
```
def fourier_objective(theta, n, M, beta):
   C=...
   phi=irfft
   p=softmax
   c=fast_conv(p)
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
   ez=np.exp(scaled)
   z=ez.sum()
   L=cmax + np.log(z)/beta
   w=ez/z
   gP=cross_conv_weights(p,w)
   mean=float(np.dot(gP,p))
   gPhi=p*(gP-mean)
   G=np.fft.rfft(gPhi)
   grad=np.empty(2*M)
   grad[0::2]=G[1:M+1].real
   grad[1::2]=-G[1:M+1].imag
   grad *= 2.0*n
   val=2.0*n*L
   if not finite: return 1e100, zeros
   grad[~finite]=0
   return val, grad
```
If beta <=0? Not use.

`fourier_theta_from_p(p,M)`:
```
def fourier_theta_from_p(p,M):
   n=len(p)
   phi=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); phi -= phi.mean()
   G=np.fft.rfft(phi)
   m=min(M,n//2)
   theta=np.empty(2*M)
   if m>0:
      theta[0:2*m:2] = (2.0/n)*G[1:m+1].real
      theta[1:2*m:2] = -(2.0/n)*G[1:m+1].imag
      if M>m: theta[2*m:]=0.0
   return theta
```
If M=0 returns empty.

`build_fourier_bounds(M, base=8.0)`:
```
def build_fourier_bounds(M, base=8.0):
   k=np.arange(1,M+1,dtype=float)
   b=base/(1.0+np.sqrt(k/20.0))
   b=np.maximum(b, 0.03)
   lower=np.empty(2*M); upper=np.empty(2*M)
   lower[0::2]=-b; upper[0::2]=b; lower[1::2]=-b; upper[1::2]=b
   bounds=list(zip(lower,upper))
   return bounds, lower, upper
```
For M=0 handle.

`optimize_fourier`:
```
def optimize_fourier(p_init, n, M, beta_list, deadline, base_amp=8.0, label=''):
   p=normalize_p(p_init)
   if p is None: return None, inf
   sc=exact_score_p(p,n); best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=sc
   if M<=0: return best_p,best_sc
   theta=fourier_theta_from_p(p,M)
   bounds, lower, upper = build_fourier_bounds(M, base_amp)
   theta=np.clip(theta, lower, upper)
   for beta in beta_list:
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      remaining=max(0,deadline-time.monotonic())
      # If remaining small, skip
      if remaining < 5: break
      # maxfun estimate
      mf=int(min(12000, max(300, remaining/0.05)))
      mi=int(min(5000, max(100, mf//5)))
      try:
         res=minimize(fourier_objective, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, args=(n,M,beta), options={'maxiter':mi,'maxfun':mf,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':20})
         new_theta=np.clip(res.x, lower, upper)
      except Exception:
         new_theta=theta.copy()
      p_try=theta_to_p(new_theta,n,M)
      sc_try=exact_score_p(p_try,n)
      if sc_try < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc_try; best_p=p_try.copy(); print(label+" new")
      # update p/theta if not much worse? for continuation
      if sc_try <= sc + max(1e-6, 1e-4*best_sc):
          p=p_try; sc=sc_try; theta=new_theta
      # else keep best but maybe continue from best theta? Use best p if worse.
      if sc_try > sc:
          # revert to best p theta?
          p=best_p.copy(); sc=best_sc; theta=fourier_theta_from_p(p,M); theta=clip
   return best_p,best_sc
```
If M high, recompute theta many times cost negligible. But if a beta run worsens, revert to best avoids bad. For continuation, maybe should continue from current even slight worse to reach new basin. But since L-BFGS local, if worse probably not. Could use tolerance.

However if initial p not best but potential, a beta L-BFGS might worsen LSE but exact? We use exact sc for update. It might not improve but could set up next beta. Hard. We can keep `theta = new_theta` regardless if finite. Because high beta continuation often needs moving. But if far worse, maybe not. Use `if sc_try < sc + 0.01: update else keep`.

def full_log_objective(theta,beta):
   n=len(theta)+1
   phi=np.empty(n); phi[0]=0; phi[1:]=theta
   p=softmax(phi)
   c=fast_conv(p); cmax; L,w; gP; mean; gPhi; grad=2n*gPhi[1:]
   val=2nL; sanitize

def logtheta_to_p(theta):

`p_to_logtheta(p, bound)`:
```
def p_to_logtheta(p, bound):
   p_safe=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
   theta=np.log(p_safe[1:]) - np.log(p_safe[0])
   return np.clip(theta, -bound, bound)
```

def make_lse_weights(c,cmax,beta,top_k):

`mirror_descent`:

```
def mirror_descent(p_init, deadline, eta_start=0.12, eta_end=0.01, beta=None, top_k=0, max_iters=100000, label=''):
   n=len(p); p=normalize; best...
   logp=np.log(p+1e-300); logp-=max; eta=eta_start; stagnant=0; rng? no.
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if now>deadline break
      c=fast_conv(p); cmax; sc=exact
      if sc<best: best; stagnant=0 else stagnant++
      if top_k>0: w=make_top_weights(c,top_k)
      else: beta_cur = beta if beta is not None else auto_lse_beta(cmax); w=make_lse
      g=cross_conv_weights(p,w); center=float(np.dot(g,p)); g_c=g-center; scale=np.max(np.abs(g_c)); if scale<... break
      base=g_c/scale
      best_trial_sc=sc; trial_p=None; chosen_eta=eta*0.25; used_factor=0.25
      # To save time, evaluate 3 factors [1,0.5,0.25] and maybe [2] if eta<1
      factors=[1.0,0.5,0.25]
      if eta > 0.3: factors.append(2.0)
      for fac in factors:
          et=eta*fac
          if et <= 1e-5: continue
          lp=logp - et*base
          lp -= lp.max()
          e=np.exp(lp); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: continue
          pt=e/s
          sc2=exact_score_p(pt,n)
          if sc2 < best_trial_sc:
              best_trial_sc=sc2; trial_p=pt; chosen_eta=et; used_factor=fac
      if trial_p is not None:
          p=trial_p; logp=np.log(p+1e-300); logp-=logp.max()
          if best_trial_sc < best_sc: print maybe
          # adapt eta
          if used_factor >= 1.0: eta=min(eta*1.2+0.005,1.5)
          elif used_factor <=0.25: eta=max(eta*0.7,eta_end)
          else: pass
          # anneal
          # perhaps decay: eta = max(eta_end, eta*0.999)
      else:
          eta=max(eta*0.7,eta_end*0.5)
          if stagnant>200 and eta<=eta_end*1.1: break
          # random kick
          if stagnant>50:
             # add small Gaussian (no rng? use deterministic sin? Could use np.random.default_rng inside? Stateless okay)
             kick = 0.05*np.sin(2*np.pi*np.arange(n)/n * 7)
             lp=logp - 0.1*kick; ... # maybe.
      # if stagnant >1000 break
   return best_p,best_sc
```

Now `make_p_candidates` top-level:
```
def build_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent):
    cands=[]
    # add normalized arrays
    # function add? no closures. We can loop tuple list?
    arrs=[]
    arrs.append(np.ones(n))
    if n>1:
       v=np.arange(n,dtype=float); arrs.append(v); arrs.append(v[::-1])
       v2=np.arange(n,dtype=float)**2; arrs.append(v2); arrs.append(v2[::-1])
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       arrs.append(np.exp(3.0*x)); arrs.append(np.exp(-3.0*x))
       arrs.append(0.5+0.5*np.sin(np.pi*x)); arrs.append(0.5+0.5*np.sin(np.pi*x)**2?)
       arrs.append(np.maximum(0.2+0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*x),1e-6)) nonneg?
       arrs.append(1.0+0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)) clip
       arrs.append(1.0+0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
       arrs.append(1.0+0.5*np.sin(4*np.pi*x)+0.3*np.sin(6*np.pi*x))
       for a,b in [(0.5,2.0),(2.0,0.5),(0.3,3.0),(0.1,1.0),(0.5,0.5)]: arrs.append((x+1e-6)**a*(1-x+1e-6)**b)
    # parent resample
    if parent is not None: arrs.append(resample_p(parent,n))
    # random fourier
    for M in [8,32,128]: for amp in [0.3,1.0,2.5]: theta random; p=theta_to_p
    for arr in arrs: p=normalize_p(arr); if p is not None: cands.append(p)
    # Remove exact duplicates? optional by hash? Skip.
    return cands
```

`process_length` details:
```
def process_length(n, parent, rng, phase_start, phase_end, seed_p, current_best_sc):
   cands=build_initial_candidates(n,rng,parent)
   if seed_p is not None:
      sr=normalize_p(seed_p)
      if len(sr)!=n: sr=resample_p(seed_p,n)
      if sr is not None: cands.insert(0,sr)
   best_p=None; best_sc=inf
   for p in cands:
      if p is None: continue
      if len(p)!=n: p=resample_p(p,n)
      if p is None: continue
      sc=exact_score_p(p,n)
      if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_p=p
   if best_p is None: best_p=np.ones(n)/n; best_sc=exact_score_p(best_p,n)
   phase_time=max(1.0, phase_end-phase_start)
   fourier_end = phase_start + 0.5*phase_time
   full_end = phase_start + 0.8*phase_time
   mirror_end = phase_end
   # Fourier M list
   p_work=best_p.copy()
   # Evaluate current best maybe print
   for M,beta_list in [(16,[2000,10000,50000]), (64,[10000,50000,200000]), (192,[20000,100000,400000])]:
      if time.monotonic() > fourier_end: break
      if M >= n//2: M = max(1,n//2-1)
      p2,sc2=optimize_fourier(p_work,n,M,beta_list,deadline=fourier_end, label=f"F4 n={n} M={M}")
      if sc2<best_sc: best_sc=sc2; best_p=p2.copy()
      p_work=best_p.copy()  # continue from best
   # If time before fourier_end, try another random start with M=64
   if time.monotonic() < fourier_end - 30:
      for cand in cands:
         # maybe pick cand if distinct from best
         if np.linalg.norm(cand-best_p) < 1e-6: continue
         p2,sc2=optimize_fourier(cand,n,64,[10000,50000],deadline=fourier_end,label='F4 random')
         update
         if sc2 < best_sc - 0.001: p_work=best_p.copy()
         break? maybe one.
   # full log
   p_work=best_p.copy()
   beta_list_full=[5000,30000,150000,600000]
   p2,sc2=optimize_full_log(p_work,beta_list_full,deadline=full_end,label=f"FL n={n}")
   update
   # If time, full log on a random/analytical cand
   if time.monotonic() < full_end - 30:
      for cand in cands:
         if norm small skip
         p2,sc2=optimize_full_log(cand, [30000,200000], deadline=full_end, label='FL rand')
         update
         if sc2 < best_sc - 0.001: p_work=best_p.copy()
         break
   # mirror polish best
   p2,sc2=mirror_descent(best_p, deadline=mirror_end, eta_start=0.15, eta_end=0.01, top_k=0, label=f"MIR n={n}")
   update
   # active mirror topk
   if time.monotonic() < mirror_end - 20:
       p2,sc2=mirror_descent(best_p, deadline=mirror_end, eta_start=0.08, eta_end=0.005, top_k=3, label='MIR3')
       update
   # final mirror if improved
   if sc2 < best_sc? best updated.
   return best_p,best_sc
```

Now, another possible algorithm: Use fractional programming / Dinkelbach with LP dual. Let's explore if can implement quick for additional improvement.

Given p, M = max c. We want find q in simplex (sum1) maximizing minimal? LP: maximize sum q s.t. conv(p,q)_k ≤ M. Since p sum1, q sum? We can solve via scipy linear programming? Variables q length n. Constraints (2n-1)x n banded. For n=7000, LP maybe large but maybe HiGHS can solve in seconds if constraints dense? Banded but scipy linprog doesn't exploit. 14k constraints, 7k vars; maybe okay with sparse `csc`. We can create sparse banded convolution matrix. But objective to update p? This LP dual could yield direction. Could be valuable but risk time. We have time, maybe implement a few solves? Let's assess.

PySparse: `scipy.sparse` allowed? Scientific libraries not explicitly but yes. Could use. Primal:
max 1^T q
s.t. A q <= M, q >=0, q sum? If no sum, unbounded scaling but constraints limit. At optimum, sum q maybe >1. Normalizing. This is what SOTA did. We can use it to generate update direction q. But solving LP for each iteration expensive. Could run once every few seconds? Need create A for fixed p? A changes with p. Actually constraints matrix depends on p (conv(p,q) linear in q with entries p). For p length n, constraint row k: sum_j p_{k-j} q_j within range. This is banded Toeplitz with values p. We can build sparse matrix efficiently using `scipy.sparse.lil` or diagonals. For n=7000, each row has up to n nonzeros, total ~n^2 ~49M too dense! Wait A is full convolution matrix (n x 2n-1) with each constraint row length up to n, total O(n^2). Not sparse enough. But constraints rows with p values; p smooth, maybe not. Previous LP approach likely for small n? For 7000, infeasible dense memory (49M floats 392MB) maybe too high. Could use dual with y variables length~2n, constraints length n involving y convolved with p, also dense? But maybe moment calculations. Too heavy. Skip.

Could implement multiplicative weights analogous to LP without solving: q ∝ 1/(p*q? )? The LP dual optimum y satisfies (p*y)_j ≥ λ. We can iterate y as softmax of constraints? This is mirror descent dual. Not.

Given constraints, first-order methods likely best.

Let's ensure final code not too long but okay. Need output only code block.

Let's write top-level functions carefully.

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def normalize_p(arr):
    if arr is None: return None
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1: a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0: return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s

def resample_p(p, n):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    if p is None: return None
    if len(p) == int(n): return p.copy()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0: return None
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(p))
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    vals = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    vals = np.clip(vals, 0.0, None)
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n) / n
    return vals / s

def softmax_from_phi(phi):
    n = len(phi)
    if n <= 0: return np.array([])
    m = float(np.max(phi))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(n) / n
    e = np.exp(phi - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n) / n
    e /= s
    # clean
    e[~np.isfinite(e)] = 0.0
    s2 = float(e.sum())
    if s2 <= 0: return np.ones(n)/n
    return e/s2
```

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0])**2])
    m = 2*n - 1
    mf = next_power_of_two(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, mf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, mf)[:m]
    # remove tiny negative
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c
```

`cross_conv_weights`:
```
def cross_conv_weights(p, w):
    n = len(p)
    if n == 1:
       # derivative: c0 = p0^2, if w length1, grad=2 w0 p0
       return 2.0*w[0]*p
    total = 3*n - 2
    mf = next_power_of_two(total)
    rp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], mf)
    rw = np.fft.rfft(w, mf)
    out = np.fft.irfft(rp*rw, mf)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return 2.0*np.where(out > 0.0, out, 0.0)
```
Check n=1 w len1, slice n-1:2n-1 =0:1 works if total=1? p[::-1] len1, w len1, M=1, output [n-1:2n-1]=[0:1], okay; no need special but can.

`exact_score_p`: as above.

`theta_to_p`:
```
def theta_to_p(theta, n, M, clip_phi=None):
   m = min(M, n//2)
   C = np.zeros(n//2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
   if m > 0:
       A = theta[0:2*m:2]
       B = theta[1:2*m:2]
       C[1:m+1] = 0.5 * float(n) * (A - 1j*B)
   phi = np.fft.irfft(C, n)
   if clip_phi is not None: phi = np.clip(phi, -clip_phi, clip_phi)
   return softmax_from_phi(phi)
```
If M=0 theta length 0; m 0.

`fourier_theta_from_p`:
```
def fourier_theta_from_p(p, M):
   n=len(p)
   m=min(M, max(0,n//2-1)) # maybe allow n//2? Use min.
   theta = np.zeros(2*M if M>0 else 0, dtype=np.float64)
   if m <= 0: return theta
   phi = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
   phi -= float(phi.mean())
   G = np.fft.rfft(phi)
   theta[0:2*m:2] = (2.0/float(n))*G[1:m+1].real
   theta[1:2*m:2] = -(2.0/float(n))*G[1:m+1].imag
   return theta
```
If M contains more modes than m, rest zero.

`build_fourier_bounds`:
```
def build_fourier_bounds(M, base=8.0):
   if M <= 0: return [], np.array([]), np.array([])
   k=np.arange(1,M+1,dtype=float)
   b=base/(1.0+np.sqrt(k/20.0))
   b=np.maximum(b,0.02)
   lower=np.empty(2*M); upper=np.empty(2*M)
   lower[0::2] = -b; upper[0::2]=b; lower[1::2] = -b; upper[1::2]=b
   bounds = [(-float(lower[i]), float(upper[i])) for i in range(2*M)]
   return bounds, lower, upper
```

`fourier_objective`:
Need use `M` maybe less than len theta//2. If M=0 return inf? Not called.
```
def fourier_objective(theta, n, M, beta):
   n=int(n); M=int(M)
   if M<=0 or len(theta)<2*M:
      return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)
   C=np.zeros(n//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
   m=min(M, n//2)
   A=theta[0:2*m:2]; B=theta[1:2*m:2]
   C[1:m+1]=0.5*float(n)*(A - 1j*B)
   phi=np.fft.irfft(C,n)
   p=softmax_from_phi(phi)
   c=fast_conv(p)
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(cmax): return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)
   scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
   ez=np.exp(scaled)
   z=float(ez.sum())
   if z<=0 or not np.isfinite(z):
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); ez=np.zeros(len(c)); ez[idx]=1.0; z=1.0
   L=cmax + float(np.log(z))/beta
   w=ez/z
   gP=cross_conv_weights(p,w)
   mean=float(np.dot(gP,p))
   gPhi=p*(gP-mean)
   G=np.fft.rfft(gPhi)
   grad=np.zeros(2*M, dtype=np.float64)
   if m>0:
      grad[0:2*m:2]=G[1:m+1].real
      grad[1:2*m:2]=-G[1:m+1].imag
      if 2*m < 2*M:
          grad[2*m:]=0.0
   grad *= float(2.0*n)
   val=float(2.0*n*L)
   if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e100
   grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0
   return val, grad
```
If n//2 maybe 0? n large.

`optimize_fourier`: As above. Need use `time` module top. Ensure no closures.
```
def optimize_fourier(p_init, n, M, beta_list, deadline, base_amp=8.0, label=''):
    p=normalize_p(p_init); n=int(n)
    if p is None or len(p)!=n:
       p=resample_p(p_init,n)
    if p is None: p=np.ones(n)/n
    # reduce M
    M = max(1, min(int(M), n//2 - 1 if n>2 else 1))
    sc=exact_score_p(p,n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=sc
    if M <=0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
       return best_p,best_sc
    theta=fourier_theta_from_p(p,M)
    bounds, lower, upper = build_fourier_bounds(M, base_amp)
    if len(theta)!=2*M:
       theta2=np.zeros(2*M); m=min(len(theta),2*M); theta2[:m]=theta[:m]; theta=theta2
    theta=np.clip(theta, lower, upper)
    print(f"  {label} start best={sc:.8f} M={M}")
    for beta in beta_list:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 5: break
       mf=int(min(12000, max(300, remaining/0.05)))
       mi=int(min(5000, max(100, mf//5)))
       try:
          res=minimize(fourier_objective, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, args=(n,M,beta), options={'maxiter':mi,'maxfun':mf,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':20})
          new_theta=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
       except Exception:
          new_theta=theta.copy()
       if new_theta.size != 2*M:
          new_theta=np.clip(theta, lower, upper)
       new_theta=np.clip(new_theta, lower, upper)
       p_try=theta_to_p(new_theta,n,M)
       p_try=normalize_p(p_try)
       if p_try is None:
          continue
       sc_try=exact_score_p(p_try,n)
       if not np.isfinite(sc_try): sc_try=1e100
       if sc_try < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc_try; best_p=p_try.copy(); print(f"  {label} beta={beta} new best {best_sc:.8f}")
       # update working
       if sc_try < sc + max(1e-7, 1e-4*best_sc):
           p=p_try; sc=sc_try; theta=new_theta
       else:
           # Keep lower-scoring? maybe best
           p=best_p.copy(); sc=best_sc
           theta=np.clip(fourier_theta_from_p(p,M), lower, upper)
    return best_p,best_sc
```

def logtheta_to_p(theta):

`p_to_logtheta`:
```
def p_to_logtheta(p, bound):
   p=normalize_p(p)
   if p is None: return np.zeros(0)
   p_safe=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
   theta=np.log(p_safe[1:]) - np.log(p_safe[0])
   return np.clip(theta, -bound, bound)
```

`optimize_full_log`:
```
def optimize_full_log(p_init, beta_list, deadline, log_bound=16.0, label=''):
   p=normalize_p(p_init)
   if p is None: p=np.ones(len(p_init) if p_init is not None else 1)/(...)
   n=len(p); sc=...
   if n<=2: return
   log_bound = min(log_bound, 25.0)
   # handle if p[0] zero? p_to
   for beta in beta_list:
     if now>=deadline break
     remaining=...
     if remaining<5 break
     theta=p_to_logtheta(p, log_bound)
     bounds=[(-log_bound, log_bound)] * (n-1)
     mf=int(min(20000, max(300, remaining/0.05)))
     mi=int(min(10000, max(100, mf//5)))
     try residual... args=(beta,)
     newt=clip(res.x,...)
     p_try=logtheta_to_p(newt); normalize
     sc_try=exact
     update
     if sc_try < sc + max(1e-7,1e-4*best_sc): p=p_try; sc=sc_try
     else: p=best_p.copy(); sc=best_sc
   return best
```

Mirror weights helpers:
```
def lse_weights(c, cmax, beta):
   scaled=beta*(c-cmax); ez=np.exp(scaled); s=ez.sum(); if s<=0: ...; return ez/s
def top_k_weights(c,k):
   k=int(k)
   if k<=0: return None
   k=min(k,len(c))
   idx=np.argpartition(c, len(c)-k)[-k:]
   w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[idx]=1.0/k; return w
```

`mirror_descent`:
Need `max_iters` and stagnation. Use `label`, `print_every` maybe.
```
def mirror_descent(p_init, deadline, eta_start=0.15, eta_end=0.01, beta=None, top_k=0, max_iters=200000, label='', print_every=1000):
    n=len(p_init); p=normalize_p(p_init)
    if p is None: p=np.ones(n)/n
    sc=exact_score_p(p,n); best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=sc
    logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); logp=logp-logp.max()
    eta=float(eta_start); stagnant=0; last_print=0
    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c=fast_conv(p); cmax=float(np.max(c)); sc=2.0*n*cmax # p sum1, but if p normalized sum1; still compute S? exact_score_p does sum, but faster.
       # Use exact_score_p to handle sum? p sum1.
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
           best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy(); stagnant=0; print...
       else:
           stagnant += 1
       if top_k and top_k > 0:
           w=top_k_weights(c, top_k)
       else:
           beta_cur = float(beta) if beta is not None else auto_lse_beta(cmax)
           w=lse_weights(c,cmax,beta_cur)
       g=cross_conv_weights(p,w)
       center=float(np.dot(g,p))
       g_c=g-center
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(g_c)))
       if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-30:
           break
       base=g_c/scale
       best_trial_sc=sc; trial_p=None; chosen_factor=0.25
       # candidate factors: maybe choose dynamically
       factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25)
       if eta > 0.25: factors = factors + (2.0,)
       for fac in factors:
           et=eta*fac
           if et < 1e-5: continue
           lp=logp - et*base
           lp = lp - float(np.max(lp))
           e=np.exp(lp); s=float(e.sum())
           if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): continue
           pt=e/s
           sc2=fast_conv(pt); c2=float(np.max(sc2)); sc2_score=2.0*n*c2  # pt sum1
           if sc2_score < best_trial_sc:
               best_trial_sc=sc2_score; trial_p=pt; chosen_factor=fac
       if trial_p is not None:
           p=trial_p
           logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); logp=logp-logp.max()
           if chosen_factor >= 0.99:
               eta = min(float(eta_start), eta*1.25 + 0.002)
           elif chosen_factor <= 0.26:
               eta = max(eta_end, eta*0.75)
           # mild anneal
           eta = max(eta_end, eta*0.9998)
       else:
           eta = max(eta_end*0.5, eta*0.75)
           if stagnant > 200:
               # random/smooth kick
               lap_length = n
               t=np.arange(lap_length, dtype=float)
               kick = 0.05*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t/lap_length*7 + 0.3)
               lp=logp - 0.1*kick
               lp=lp-lp.max(); e=np.exp(lp); s=e.sum()
               if s>0:
                   p=e/s
                   logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); logp-=logp.max()
               if stagnant > 500:
                   break
       if it % 2000 == 0:
           print(f" {label} mir iter {it} cur {sc:.8f} best {best_sc:.8f} eta {eta:.4f}")
    return best_p, best_sc
```
In candidate score, we used p sum1. But if p from e/s sum1. Good. For consistency with exact_score_p, use `float(np.max(fast_conv(pt)))` times 2n, no sum. Fine.

Need `auto_lse_beta`:
```
def auto_lse_beta(cmax):
   if cmax <= 0 or not finite: return 1e5
   return float(min(1e7, max(1000.0, 20.0/cmax)))
```
If cmax=1e-4, beta=200k.

The mirror line search evaluates up to 4 candidates every iter. If deadline long, too many? 200k iterations could be 800k FFT, too slow. Need limit based on time? Since each iter maybe several ms, miniters in 2400s maybe 100k. But for polishing, if no improvements, break after 500 stagnant. For from scratch, improvements many? Could be. We can cap max_iters per call maybe 50000. But if time, no. Use `max_iters=50000`. In process phases with deadline e.g., 100s, it will stop by time. 50000*4 FFT=200k FFT; maybe 20-60s. okay.

Unary: `factors = factors + (2.0,)` creates tuple; okay.

Potential bug: In `sc=2.0*n*cmax`, if p not exactly sum1 due normalize? normalized sum1 but maybe tiny. Use exact_score_p at start and candidate to be safe? exact_score sums p each time (cheap). But fast_conv + sum. Candidate: `sc2 = exact_score_p(pt,n)` maybe more robust. The sum is 1 but use exact. We'll use exact_score_p to avoid issues; extra sum small. In current sc after c, we can compute `sc = 2n cmax` since p sum1. Fine. Candidate use exact.

Now, `build_initial_candidates`:
Need avoid duplicate large arrays? fine.
```
def build_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent):
   n=int(n)
   arrs=[]
   arrs.append(np.ones(n))
   if n>1:
      v=np.arange(n,dtype=float); arrs.append(v); arrs.append(v[::-1])
      v2=v*v; arrs.append(v2); arrs.append(v2[::-1])
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      arrs.append(np.exp(3*x)); arrs.append(np.exp(-3*x))
      arrs.append(0.2 + 0.8*x); arrs.append(0.2+0.8*(1-x))
      arrs.append(np.maximum(1.0 + 0.7*np.sin(2*np.pi*x), 1e-6))
      arrs.append(np.maximum(1.0 + 0.7*np.cos(2*np.pi*x), 1e-6))
      arrs.append(np.maximum(1.0 + 0.5*np.sin(4*np.pi*x)+0.3*np.sin(6*np.pi*x),1e-6))
      for a in [0.3,0.7,1.5,2.5]:
         arrs.append((x+1e-4)**a)
         arrs.append((1-x+1e-4)**a)
      # parent
      if parent is not None:
         pr=resample_p(parent,n)
         if pr is not None: arrs.append(pr)
   # random smooth
   for M in [8, 32]:
      for amp in [0.1,0.4,1.0,2.0]:
         m = max(1, min(M, n//2-1 if n>2 else 1))
         theta=rng.normal(0.0, amp, 2*m)
         p=theta_to_p(theta,n,m)
         arrs.append(p)
   cands=[]
   for arr in arrs:
      p=normalize_p(arr)
      if p is not None: cands.append(p)
   # Also small perturbations of parent for local basins
   if parent is not None:
      pr=resample_p(parent,n)
      if pr is not None:
         logp=np.log(np.maximum(pr,1e-300)); logp-=logp.mean()
         for amp in [0.05,0.2,0.5]:
            for M in [8,32]:
               m=max(1,min(M,n//2-1 if n>2 else 1))
               pert_theta=rng.normal(0,amp,2*m)
               # add to parent theta? Need parent theta amplitude: G=rft(logp)
               G=np.fft.rfft(logp)
               base_theta = np.zeros(2*m)
               base_theta[0:2*m:2] = (2/n)*G[1:m+1].real
               base_theta[1:2*m:2] = -(2/n)*G[1:m+1].imag
               pert = base_theta + pert_theta
               p=theta_to_p(pert,n,m)
               if p is not None: cands.append(p)
   return cands
```
Need no closures. This may create many candidates (~10+ random 16+ parent 8 =40), okay.

But `rng.normal` returns shape. Use `m` variable.

`process_length`:
```
def process_length(n, parent, rng, phase_start, phase_end, seed_p):
    n=int(n)
    cands=build_initial_candidates(n,rng,parent)
    if seed_p is not None:
       sp=resample_p(seed_p,n)
       if sp is not None: cands=[sp]+cands  # list concat
    cand_eval=[]
    for i,p in enumerate(cands):
       sc=exact_score_p(p,n)
       if not finite: sc=1e100
       cand_eval.append((sc,i,p))
    cand_eval.sort()
    if not cand_eval:
       best_p=np.ones(n)/n; best_sc=exact
    else:
       best_sc, _, best_p = cand_eval[0]
    # choose work nonuniform
    work_p=best_p
    for sc,i,p in cand_eval:
       if sc <= best_sc + 0.2:
          diff=float(np.linalg.norm(p-best_p))
          if diff > 1e-4:
             work_p=p.copy(); break
    phase_time=max(1.0, phase_end-phase_start)
    pre_end = phase_start + min(60.0, 0.08*phase_time)
    # pre mirror if any time
    if pre_end > time.monotonic() + 5:
       p2,sc2=mirror_descent(work_p, deadline=pre_end, eta_start=0.35, eta_end=0.05, top_k=7, max_iters=20000, label=f"PRE n={n}")
       if sc2 < best_sc: best_sc=sc2; best_p=p2.copy()
       work_p=best_p.copy()
    fourier_end = phase_start + min(0.55*phase_time, (phase_end-phase_start)*0.5 + (pre_end-phase_start)?)
```
Simpler: compute relative from phase_start, but pre may consume. Use:

mirror_end = phase_end

```

But if phase short 100s, pre 60 leaves little. Maybe pre_end min 30, 0.05 phase. Use `pre_end = phase_start + min(45.0, 0.06*phase_time)`.

`process_length`:
```
def process_length(n, parent, rng, phase_start, phase_end, seed_p):
    n = int(n)
    cands = build_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent)
    if seed_p is not None:
        sp = resample_p(seed_p, n)
        if sp is not None:
            cands = [sp] + cands
    cand_eval = []
    for i, p in enumerate(cands):
        if p is None:
            continue
        if len(p) != n:
            p = resample_p(p, n)
            if p is None:
                continue
        sc = exact_score_p(p, n)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            sc = 1e100
        cand_eval.append((sc, i, p))
    if not cand_eval:
        best_p = np.ones(n)/n
        best_sc = exact_score_p(best_p, n)
    else:
        cand_eval.sort()
        best_sc, _, best_p = cand_eval[0]
    work_p = best_p.copy()
    # find nonuniform
    for sc, i, p in cand_eval:
        if sc <= best_sc + 0.25:
            diff = float(np.linalg.norm(p - best_p))
            if diff > 1e-4:
                work_p = p.copy()
                break
    phase_time = max(1.0, float(phase_end - phase_start))
    pre_end = phase_start + min(45.0, 0.06 * phase_time)
    if pre_end > time.monotonic() + 3.0:
        p2, sc2 = mirror_descent(work_p, deadline=pre_end, eta_start=0.35, eta_end=0.05, top_k=7, max_iters=20000, label=f"PRE n={n}")
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2; best_p = p2.copy()
        work_p = best_p.copy()
    fourier_end = phase_start + 0.55 * phase_time
    if fourier_end > phase_end: fourier_end=phase_end
    M_configs = [(16, [2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0]), (64, [10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0]), (192, [20000.0, 100000.0, 400000.0])]
    p_work = best_p.copy()
    for Mcfg, betas in M_configs:
        if time.monotonic() >= fourier_end:
            break
        p_work, sc_work = optimize_fourier(p_work, n, Mcfg, betas, deadline=fourier_end, label=f"F n={n} M={Mcfg}")
        if sc_work < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_work; best_p = p_work.copy()
        p_work = best_p.copy()
    # second candidate
    if time.monotonic() < fourier_end - 15:
        for sc, i, p in cand_eval:
            if p is best_p: # array equality? p is same object? Might be false. Use diff.
                continue
            if sc <= best_sc + 0.25:
                diff = float(np.linalg.norm(p - best_p))
                if diff > 1e-4:
                    p2, sc2 = optimize_fourier(p, n, 64, [10000.0, 50000.0], deadline=fourier_end, label=f"FR n={n}")
                    if sc2 < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc2; best_p = p2.copy()
                    break
    full_end = phase_start + 0.80 * phase_time
    if full_end > phase_end: full_end=phase_end
    if time.monotonic() < full_end:
        if n > 12000:
            full_betas=[20000.0, 100000.0, 300000.0]
        elif n > 8000:
            full_betas=[10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0, 800000.0]
        else:
            full_betas=[5000.0, 30000.0, 150000.0, 600000.0, 1500000.0]
        p2, sc2 = optimize_full_log(best_p, full_betas, deadline=full_end, label=f"FL n={n}")
        if sc2 < best_sc: update
    if time.monotonic() < full_end - 25:
       for sc,i,p in cand_eval:
          diff...
          if ...:
             p2,sc2=optimize_full_log(p, full_betas[:2], deadline=full_end, label=f"FLR n={n}")
             update; break
    mirror_end = phase_end
    p2,sc2=mirror_descent(best_p, deadline=mirror_end, eta_start=0.12, eta_end=0.01, top_k=0, max_iters=40000, label=f"ML n={n}")
    update
    if time.monotonic() < mirror_end - 15:
       p2,sc2=mirror_descent(best_p, deadline=mirror_end, eta_start=0.10, eta_end=0.003, top_k=5, max_iters=40000, label=f"M5 n={n}")
       update
    if time.monotonic() < mirror_end - 5:
       p2,sc2=mirror_descent(best_p, deadline=mirror_end, eta_start=0.06, eta_end=0.002, top_k=1, max_iters=20000, label=f"M1 n={n}")
       update
    return best_p,best_sc
```

Now, `full_log_objective`: We should project gradient? We compute gPhi = p*(gP-mean). That's gradient for all n variables. But θ excludes v0. v0 fixed at 0. If we don't fix v0 by subtracting mean, the gradient wrt v_i (i>0) is gPhi_i? Let's verify parameterization: φ_0=0, φ_i=θ_i for i>0. p=softmax(φ). The gradient wrt θ_i = ∂L/∂φ_i = gPhi_i. The derivative wrt φ_0 = gPhi_0 is discarded; but if φ_0 fixed, that's correct because not optimizing it. But softmax normalization means adding constant to all φ changes nothing; fixing φ_0=0 removes redundancy but the feasible φ_i are not orthogonal to constant? The derivative wrt θ_i with φ_0 fixed is indeed gPhi_i. However because φ_0 not changing, a change in θ_i changes p_i relative to all including φ0; the missing φ0 derivative is handled by fixed constant. But is there a redundancy: any p can be represented; gradient of L wrt θ_i okay. We should not subtract additional? The formula gPhi_i = p_i (g_i - g·p) is correct for φ with all components free. If we fix φ_0, the gradient for free components remains gPhi_i. Good. No need project further. The sum of gPhi not zero because φ0 missing? Actually gPhi over all n sums 0. The gradient for θ vector length n-1 does not include gPhi_0. The constant direction is removed by fixing, but derivative with respect to free θ_i includes gPhi_i, okay. However a step that adds same c to all θ_i (i>0) changes φ_0 relative? It adds c to i>0 but not (i=0), so not constant direction. Good.

For Fourier objective, we optimized amplitudes of φ with DC fixed zero. The φ obtained has mean zero? Since C[0]=0, irfft mean zero. The parameterization excludes DC. The gradient wrt amplitudes computed as sums gPhi cos/sin, where gPhi already projected to sum zero. Good.

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2 over nonnegative sequences.

The optimization is performed on normalized probability sequences p = a / sum(a),
so the objective becomes 2*n*max(conv(p,p)).  The main tools are:

- low-dimensional Fourier-band L-BFGS optimization in log-space,
- full log-softmax L-BFGS optimization,
- multiplicative / mirror-descent steps with smoothed or exact top-lag weights.

The code tries several problem lengths and uses the provided parent sequence when
available.  All returned sequences are normalized to sum 1, so the original
scale invariance of evaluate_sequence is preserved.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def max_fourier_modes(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 2:
        return 0
    if n % 2 == 0:
        return n // 2 - 1
    return n // 2


def normalize_p(arr):
    if arr is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def resample_p(p, n):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if len(p) == n:
        return p.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(p))
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    vals = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    vals = np.clip(vals, 0.0, None)
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n) / n
    return vals / s


def softmax_from_phi(phi):
    n = len(phi)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(phi))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(n) / n
    e = np.exp(phi - m)
    e[~np.isfinite(e)] = 0.0
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n) / n
    return e / s


def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0]) * float(a[0])])
    m = 2 * n - 1
    mf = next_power_of_two(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, mf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, mf)[:m]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c


def cross_conv_weights(p, w):
    n = len(p)
    total = 3 * n - 2
    mf = next_power_of_two(total)
    rp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], mf)
    rw = np.fft.rfft(w, mf)
    out = np.fft.irfft(rp * rw, mf)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return 2.0 * np.where(out > 0.0, out, 0.0)


def exact_score_p(a, n):
    S = float(np.sum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def theta_to_p(theta, n, M):
    n = int(n)
    m = min(int(M), max_fourier_modes(n))
    C = np.zeros(n // 2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
    if m > 0:
        A = theta[0:2 * m:2]
        B = theta[1:2 * m:2]
        C[1:m + 1] = 0.5 * float(n) * (A - 1j * B)
    phi = np.fft.irfft(C, n)
    return softmax_from_phi(phi)


def fourier_theta_from_p(p, M):
    n = len(p)
    M = int(M)
    m = min(M, max_fourier_modes(n))
    theta = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= 0:
        return theta
    phi = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    phi -= float(np.mean(phi))
    G = np.fft.rfft(phi)
    theta[0:2 * m:2] = (2.0 / float(n)) * G[1:m + 1].real
    theta[1:2 * m:2] = -(2.0 / float(n)) * G[1:m + 1].imag
    return theta


def build_fourier_bounds(M, base=8.0):
    M = int(M)
    if M <= 0:
        return [], np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    k = np.arange(1, M + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    b = base / (1.0 + np.sqrt(k / 20.0))
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.02)
    lower = np.empty(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    upper = np.empty(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    lower[0::2] = -b
    upper[0::2] = b
    lower[1::2] = -b
    upper[1::2] = b
    bounds = []
    for i in range(2 * M):
        bounds.append((float(lower[i]), float(upper[i])))
    return bounds, lower, upper


def fourier_objective(theta, n, M, beta):
    n = int(n)
    M = int(M)
    beta = float(beta)
    if M <= 0 or len(theta) < 2 * M:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)

    m = min(M, max_fourier_modes(n))
    C = np.zeros(n // 2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
    if m > 0:
        A = theta[0:2 * m:2]
        B = theta[1:2 * m:2]
        C[1:m + 1] = 0.5 * float(n) * (A - 1j * B)

    phi = np.fft.irfft(C, n)
    p = softmax_from_phi(phi)
    c = fast_conv(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)

    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    ez = np.exp(scaled)
    ez[~np.isfinite(ez)] = 0.0
    z = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(z) or z <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        ez = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        ez[idx] = 1.0
        z = 1.0

    L = cmax + float(np.log(z)) / beta
    w = ez / z

    gP = cross_conv_weights(p, w)
    mean_g = float(np.dot(gP, p))
    gPhi = p * (gP - mean_g)

    G = np.fft.rfft(gPhi)
    grad = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
    if m > 0:
        grad[0:2 * m:2] = G[1:m + 1].real
        grad[1:2 * m:2] = -G[1:m + 1].imag
        if 2 * m < 2 * M:
            grad[2 * m:] = 0.0
    grad *= float(2.0 * n)

    val = float(2.0 * n * L)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
    grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0
    return val, grad


def optimize_fourier(p_init, n, M, beta_list, deadline, base_amp=8.0, label=""):
    n = int(n)
    p = normalize_p(p_init)
    if p is None or len(p) != n:
        p = resample_p(p_init, n)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(n) / n

    M = max(1, min(int(M), max_fourier_modes(n)))
    sc = exact_score_p(p, n)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = sc

    if M <= 0 or time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        return best_p, best_sc

    theta = fourier_theta_from_p(p, M)
    bounds, lower, upper = build_fourier_bounds(M, base_amp)
    if theta.size != 2 * M:
        t2 = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(theta.size, 2 * M)
        t2[:m] = theta[:m]
        theta = t2
    theta = np.clip(theta, lower, upper)

    print(f"  {label} start={sc:.8f} M={M}")

    for beta in beta_list:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= float(deadline):
            break
        remaining = float(deadline) - now
        if remaining < 5.0:
            break

        if M < 32:
            cap = 7000
            tper = 0.035
        elif M < 128:
            cap = 4500
            tper = 0.055
        else:
            cap = 2800
            tper = 0.085

        maxfun = int(min(cap, max(250, remaining / max(0.001, tper * 2.5))))
        maxiter = int(min(8000, max(120, maxfun // 4)))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                fourier_objective,
                theta,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                args=(n, M, float(beta)),
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 20
                }
            )
            new_theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            new_theta = theta.copy()

        if new_theta.size != 2 * M:
            new_theta = np.clip(theta, lower, upper)
        new_theta = np.clip(new_theta, lower, upper)

        p_try = normalize_p(theta_to_p(new_theta, n, M))
        if p_try is None:
            continue
        sc_try = exact_score_p(p_try, n)
        if not np.isfinite(sc_try):
            sc_try = 1e100

        if sc_try < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_try
            best_p = p_try.copy()
            print(f"  {label} beta={beta:.1f} new best={best_sc:.8f}")

        if sc_try < sc + max(1e-7, 1e-4 * best_sc):
            p = p_try
            sc = sc_try
            theta = new_theta
        else:
            p = best_p.copy()
            sc = best_sc
            theta = np.clip(fourier_theta_from_p(p, M), lower, upper)

    return best_p, best_sc


def logtheta_to_p(theta):
    n = len(theta) + 1
    phi = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    phi[0] = 0.0
    phi[1:] = theta
    return softmax_from_phi(phi)


def p_to_logtheta(p, bound):
    p = normalize_p(p)
    if p is None:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    p_safe = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    theta = np.log(p_safe[1:]) - np.log(p_safe[0])
    return np.clip(theta, -bound, bound)


def full_log_objective(theta, beta):
    n = len(theta) + 1
    if n <= 1:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)

    phi = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    phi[0] = 0.0
    phi[1:] = theta
    p = softmax_from_phi(phi)
    c = fast_conv(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)

    beta = float(beta)
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    ez = np.exp(scaled)
    ez[~np.isfinite(ez)] = 0.0
    z = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(z) or z <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        ez = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        ez[idx] = 1.0
        z = 1.0

    L = cmax + float(np.log(z)) / beta
    w = ez / z

    gP = cross_conv_weights(p, w)
    mean_g = float(np.dot(gP, p))
    gPhi = p * (gP - mean_g)

    grad = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    grad[:] = 2.0 * float(n) * gPhi[1:]

    val = float(2.0 * n * L)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
    grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0
    return val, grad


def optimize_full_log(p_init, beta_list, deadline, log_bound=18.0, label=""):
    p = normalize_p(p_init)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(2048) / 2048
    n = len(p)
    sc = exact_score_p(p, n)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = sc

    if n <= 2 or time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        return best_p, best_sc

    log_bound = min(float(log_bound), 22.0)

    for beta in beta_list:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= float(deadline):
            break
        remaining = float(deadline) - now
        if remaining < 5.0:
            break

        if n < 7000:
            cap = 6000
            tper = 0.040
        elif n < 12000:
            cap = 4000
            tper = 0.060
        else:
            cap = 2500
            tper = 0.090

        maxfun = int(min(cap, max(250, remaining / max(0.001, tper * 2.5))))
        maxiter = int(min(10000, max(120, maxfun // 4)))

        theta = p_to_logtheta(p, log_bound)
        bounds = [(-log_bound, log_bound)] * (n - 1)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                full_log_objective,
                theta,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                args=(float(beta),),
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 20
                }
            )
            newt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            newt = theta.copy()

        newt = np.clip(newt, -log_bound, log_bound)
        p_try = normalize_p(logtheta_to_p(newt))
        if p_try is None or len(p_try) != n:
            continue

        sc_try = exact_score_p(p_try, n)
        if not np.isfinite(sc_try):
            sc_try = 1e100

        if sc_try < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_try
            best_p = p_try.copy()
            print(f"  {label} beta={beta:.1f} new best={best_sc:.8f}")

        if sc_try < sc + max(1e-7, 1e-4 * best_sc):
            p = p_try
            sc = sc_try
        else:
            p = best_p.copy()
            sc = best_sc

    return best_p, best_sc


def auto_lse_beta(cmax):
    cmax = float(cmax)
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 1e5
    return float(min(1e7, max(1e3, 20.0 / cmax)))


def lse_weights(c, cmax, beta):
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        w = lse_weights(c, cmax, 1e5)
        return w
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    ez = np.exp(scaled)
    ez[~np.isfinite(ez)] = 0.0
    z = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(z) or z <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    return ez / z


def top_k_weights(c, k):
    k = int(k)
    if k <= 0:
        return None
    k = min(k, len(c))
    idx = np.argpartition(c, len(c) - k)[-k:]
    w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def mirror_descent(p_init, deadline, eta_start=0.15, eta_end=0.01, beta=None,
                   top_k=0, max_iters=50000, label=""):
    p = normalize_p(p_init)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = len(p)
    sc = exact_score_p(p, n)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = sc

    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    logp = logp - float(np.max(logp))
    eta = float(eta_start)
    stale = 0

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break

        c = fast_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            stale = 0
            if sc < best_sc:
                if it % 500 == 0 or it < 20:
                    print(f"  {label} iter={it} cur={sc:.8f} best={best_sc:.8f} eta={eta:.4f}")
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = p.copy()
        else:
            stale += 1

        if top_k is not None and int(top_k) > 0:
            w = top_k_weights(c, int(top_k))
        else:
            b = auto_lse_beta(cmax) if beta is None else float(beta)
            w = lse_weights(c, cmax, b)

        g = cross_conv_weights(p, w)
        center = float(np.dot(g, p))
        gc = g - center
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-30:
            break

        base = gc / scale

        best_trial_sc = sc
        best_trial_p = None
        chosen_factor = 0.25

        factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25)
        if eta > 0.25:
            factors = factors + (2.0,)

        for fac in factors:
            et = eta * fac
            if et < 1e-5:
                continue
            lp = logp - et * base
            lp = lp - float(np.max(lp))
            e = np.exp(lp)
            e[~np.isfinite(e)] = 0.0
            s = float(e.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                continue
            pt = e / s
            sc2 = exact_score_p(pt, n)
            if not np.isfinite(sc2):
                continue
            if sc2 < best_trial_sc:
                best_trial_sc = sc2
                best_trial_p = pt
                chosen_factor = fac

        if best_trial_p is not None:
            p = best_trial_p
            logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            logp = logp - float(np.max(logp))
            if chosen_factor >= 0.99:
                eta = min(1.8, eta * 1.25 + 0.002)
            elif chosen_factor <= 0.26:
                eta = max(eta_end, eta * 0.75)
            eta = max(eta_end, eta * 0.9999)
        else:
            eta = max(0.5 * eta_end, eta * 0.75)
            if stale > 120:
                t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
                kick = 0.06 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t / max(1.0, float(n)) * 7.0 + 0.35)
                lp = logp - 0.12 * kick
                lp = lp - float(np.max(lp))
                e = np.exp(lp)
                e[~np.isfinite(e)] = 0.0
                s = float(e.sum())
                if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0:
                    p = e / s
                    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                    logp = logp - float(np.max(logp))
                if stale > 500:
                    break

    return best_p, best_sc


def random_theta_p(n, M, amp, rng):
    m = min(int(M), max_fourier_modes(n))
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(n) / n
    theta = rng.normal(0.0, float(amp), 2 * m)
    return theta_to_p(theta, n, m)


def build_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent):
    n = int(n)
    arrs = []
    arrs.append(np.ones(n))

    if n > 1:
        v = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        arrs.append(v)
        arrs.append(v[::-1])
        v2 = v * v
        arrs.append(v2)
        arrs.append(v2[::-1])

        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        arrs.append(np.exp(3.0 * x))
        arrs.append(np.exp(-3.0 * x))
        arrs.append(0.2 + 0.8 * x)
        arrs.append(0.2 + 0.8 * (1.0 - x))
        arrs.append(np.maximum(1.0 + 0.7 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-6))
        arrs.append(np.maximum(1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-6))
        arrs.append(np.maximum(1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.3 * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-6))

        for a in [0.3, 0.7, 1.5, 2.5]:
            arrs.append((x + 1e-4) ** a)
            arrs.append((1.0 - x + 1e-4) ** a)

        if parent is not None:
            pr = resample_p(parent, n)
            if pr is not None:
                arrs.append(pr)

    for M in [8, 32]:
        for amp in [0.1, 0.4, 1.0, 2.0]:
            p = random_theta_p(n, M, amp, rng)
            if p is not None:
                arrs.append(p)

    if parent is not None:
        pr = resample_p(parent, n)
        if pr is not None:
            logp = np.log(np.maximum(pr, 1e-300))
            logp = logp - float(np.mean(logp))
            G = np.fft.rfft(logp)
            for amp in [0.05, 0.2, 0.5]:
                for M in [8, 32]:
                    m = min(M, max_fourier_modes(n))
                    if m <= 0:
                        continue
                    base_theta = np.zeros(2 * m, dtype=np.float64)
                    base_theta[0:2 * m:2] = (2.0 / float(n)) * G[1:m + 1].real
                    base_theta[1:2 * m:2] = -(2.0 / float(n)) * G[1:m + 1].imag
                    pert_theta = base_theta + rng.normal(0.0, amp, 2 * m)
                    p = theta_to_p(pert_theta, n, m)
                    if p is not None:
                        arrs.append(p)

    cands = []
    for arr in arrs:
        p = normalize_p(arr)
        if p is not None:
            cands.append(p)
    return cands


def process_length(n, parent, rng, phase_start, phase_end, seed_p):
    n = int(n)
    cands = build_initial_candidates(n, rng, parent)
    if seed_p is not None:
        sp = resample_p(seed_p, n)
        if sp is not None:
            cands = [sp] + cands

    cand_eval = []
    for i, p in enumerate(cands):
        if p is None:
            continue
        if len(p) != n:
            p = resample_p(p, n)
            if p is None:
                continue
        sc = exact_score_p(p, n)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            sc = 1e100
        cand_eval.append((sc, i, p))

    if not cand_eval:
        best_p = np.ones(n) / n
        best_sc = exact_score_p(best_p, n)
    else:
        cand_eval.sort()
        best_sc, _, best_p = cand_eval[0]

    work_p = best_p.copy()
    for sc, i, p in cand_eval:
        if sc <= best_sc + 0.25:
            diff = float(np.linalg.norm(p - best_p))
            if diff > 1e-4:
                work_p = p.copy()
                break

    phase_time = max(1.0, float(phase_end - phase_start))

    pre_end = phase_start + min(45.0, 0.06 * phase_time)
    if pre_end > time.monotonic() + 3.0:
        p2, sc2 = mirror_descent(
            work_p,
            deadline=pre_end,
            eta_start=0.35,
            eta_end=0.05,
            top_k=7,
            max_iters=20000,
            label=f"PRE n={n}"
        )
        if p2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2.copy()
        work_p = best_p.copy()

    fourier_end = phase_start + 0.55 * phase_time
    if fourier_end > phase_end:
        fourier_end = phase_end

    p_work = best_p.copy()
    M_configs = [
        (16, [2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0]),
        (64, [10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0]),
        (192, [20000.0, 100000.0, 400000.0])
    ]

    for Mcfg, betas in M_configs:
        if time.monotonic() >= fourier_end:
            break
        p_work, sc_work = optimize_fourier(
            p_work,
            n,
            Mcfg,
            betas,
            deadline=fourier_end,
            label=f"F n={n} M={Mcfg}"
        )
        if sc_work < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_work
            best_p = p_work.copy()
        p_work = best_p.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < fourier_end - 15.0:
        for sc, i, p in cand_eval:
            diff = float(np.linalg.norm(p - best_p))
            if diff <= 1e-4:
                continue
            if sc <= best_sc + 0.25:
                p2, sc2 = optimize_fourier(
                    p,
                    n,
                    64,
                    [10000.0, 50000.0],
                    deadline=fourier_end,
                    label=f"FR n={n}"
                )
                if sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                break

    full_end = phase_start + 0.80 * phase_time
    if full_end > phase_end:
        full_end = phase_end

    if n > 12000:
        full_betas = [20000.0, 100000.0, 300000.0]
    elif n > 8000:
        full_betas = [10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0, 800000.0]
    else:
        full_betas = [5000.0, 30000.0, 150000.0, 600000.0, 1500000.0]

    if time.monotonic() < full_end:
        p2, sc2 = optimize_full_log(
            best_p,
            full_betas,
            deadline=full_end,
            label=f"FL n={n}"
        )
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < full_end - 25.0:
        for sc, i, p in cand_eval:
            diff = float(np.linalg.norm(p - best_p))
            if diff <= 1e-4:
                continue
            if sc <= best_sc + 0.25:
                p2, sc2 = optimize_full_log(
                    p,
                    full_betas[:2],
                    deadline=full_end,
                    label=f"FLR n={n}"
                )
                if sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                break

    mirror_end = phase_end

    p2, sc2 = mirror_descent(
        best_p,
        deadline=mirror_end,
        eta_start=0.12,
        eta_end=0.01,
        top_k=0,
        max_iters=40000,
        label=f"ML n={n}"
    )
    if p2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc2
        best_p = p2.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < mirror_end - 15.0:
        p2, sc2 = mirror_descent(
            best_p,
            deadline=mirror_end,
            eta_start=0.10,
            eta_end=0.003,
            top_k=5,
            max_iters=40000,
            label=f"M5 n={n}"
        )
        if p2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < mirror_end - 5.0:
        p2, sc2 = mirror_descent(
            best_p,
            deadline=mirror_end,
            eta_start=0.06,
            eta_end=0.002,
            top_k=1,
            max_iters=20000,
            label=f"M1 n={n}"
        )
        if p2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2.copy()

    return best_p, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    finalize_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, total - 30.0)
    search_end = t0 + max(120.0, total - 150.0)
    search_budget = max(60.0, search_end - t0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        if raw is not None:
            p = normalize_p(raw)
            if p is not None:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        base_n = 6962
        best_p = np.ones(base_n) / base_n
        best_sc = exact_score_p(best_p, base_n)
    else:
        base_n = max(2048, min(12288, len(parent)))
        if len(parent) != base_n:
            best_p = resample_p(parent, base_n)
            if best_p is None:
                best_p = np.ones(base_n) / base_n
        else:
            best_p = parent.copy()
        best_sc = exact_score_p(best_p, base_n)

    lengths = []
    candidate_lengths = [
        base_n,
        6962,
        8192,
        max(2048, base_n // 2),
        min(12288, base_n * 2)
    ]
    for n_try in candidate_lengths:
        if 2048 <= int(n_try) <= 12288 and int(n_try) not in lengths:
            lengths.append(int(n_try))

    prio = []
    for n in lengths:
        rank = 0 if n == base_n else 1
        prio.append((rank, abs(n - 6962), n))
    prio.sort()
    sorted_lengths = []
    for item in prio:
        sorted_lengths.append(item[2])
        if len(sorted_lengths) >= 3:
            break

    if len(sorted_lengths) == 1:
        fractions = [1.0]
    elif len(sorted_lengths) == 2:
        fractions = [0.62, 0.38]
    else:
        fractions = [0.55, 0.29, 0.16]

    cum = 0.0
    for idx, n in enumerate(sorted_lengths):
        prev_cum = cum
        cum += fractions[idx]
        phase_start = t0 + search_budget * max(0.0, prev_cum)
        phase_end = t0 + search_budget * cum
        if phase_end > search_end:
            phase_end = search_end

        if phase_end <= time.monotonic() + 2.0:
            break

        seed_p = best_p
        if len(seed_p) != n:
            seed_p = resample_p(seed_p, n)

        print(f"\n=== Length n={n}, phase [{time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s, "
              f"phase_end={phase_end-t0:.0f}s], current best={best_sc:.8f}")

        p_len, sc_len = process_length(
            n,
            parent,
            rng,
            phase_start,
            phase_end,
            seed_p
        )

        if p_len is not None and sc_len < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_len
            best_p = p_len
            print(f"  global updated: {best_sc:.8f} n={len(best_p)}")

    # Final polishing stage.
    if time.monotonic() < finalize_deadline - 60.0:
        final_betas = [300000.0, 800000.0]
        p2, sc2 = optimize_full_log(
            best_p,
            final_betas,
            deadline=finalize_deadline - 60.0,
            label="FINAL-FL"
        )
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2
            print(f"  final full-log updated: {best_sc:.8f}")

    if time.monotonic() < finalize_deadline - 35.0:
        p2, sc2 = mirror_descent(
            best_p,
            deadline=finalize_deadline - 35.0,
            eta_start=0.10,
            eta_end=0.003,
            top_k=5,
            max_iters=30000,
            label="FINAL-M5"
        )
        if p2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2
            print(f"  final top5 mirror updated: {best_sc:.8f}")

    if time.monotonic() < finalize_deadline - 12.0:
        p2, sc2 = mirror_descent(
            best_p,
            deadline=finalize_deadline - 12.0,
            eta_start=0.06,
            eta_end=0.0015,
            top_k=1,
            max_iters=20000,
            label="FINAL-M1"
        )
        if p2 is not None and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2
            print(f"  final top1 mirror updated: {best_sc:.8f}")

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(out_arr), out_arr, 0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024)

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float(best_sc)

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print(f"INTERNAL BEST: {best_sc:.10f}")
    print(f"VERIFIED EVAL: {verified:.10f}")
    print(f"LENGTH: {len(out)}")
    print(f"TIME USED: {time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s / {budget_s:.0f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return out
```
